The History of Italy, Vol. 6 (Francesco Guicciardini (Austin Parke Goddard, trans.), 1561)
==========================================================
Guicciardini's Storia d'Italia (written 1537-40, first printed 1561) - the first 'modern' analytical history; the Italian wars as the tragedy of a divided peninsula. Goddard's English translation (1753-61, 10 vols.). Vol. 6 of 10.
